```
    31→def show_detector_progress(state: dict):
    32→    """Show per-detector progress bars — the heartbeat of a scan."""
    33→    return presentation_mod.show_detector_progress(
    34→        state,
    35→        state_mod=state_mod,
    36→        narrative_mod=narrative_constants_mod,
    37→        registry_mod=registry_mod,
    38→        colorize_fn=colorize,
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→def _dimension_bar(score: float, *, bar_len: int = 15) -> str:
    43→    """Render a score bar consistent with scan detector bars."""
    44→    return presentation_mod.dimension_bar(score, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
    45→
    46→
    47→def scorecard_dimension_entries(
    48→    state: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    51→) -> list[dict]:
    52→    """Return scorecard rows with presentation-friendly metadata."""
    53→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    54→    subjective_by_name = {
    55→        entry["name"]: entry
    56→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(
    57→            state,
    58→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    59→        )
    60→    }
    61→    entries: list[dict] = []
    62→    for name, data in rows:
    63→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    64→        subjective_meta = subjective_by_name.get(name)
    65→        is_subjective = subjective_meta is not None
    66→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    67→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    68→        issues = int(data.get("failing", 0))
    69→        checks = int(data.get("checks", 0))
    70→        placeholder = bool(subjective_meta.get("placeholder")) if subjective_meta else False
    71→        not_scanned = bool(
    72→            not is_subjective and not detectors and checks == 0
    73→        )
    74→        carried_forward = bool(
    75→            not is_subjective and data.get("carried_forward")
    76→        )
    77→        dim_key = str(subjective_meta.get("dimension_key", "")) if subjective_meta else ""
    78→        stale = bool(subjective_meta.get("stale")) if subjective_meta else False
    79→        cli_keys = (
    80→            list(subjective_meta.get("cli_keys", []))
    81→            if subjective_meta
    82→            else scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data)
    83→        )
    84→        entries.append(
    85→            {
    86→                "name": name,
    87→                "score": score,
    88→                "strict": strict,
    89→                "failing": issues,
    90→                "checks": checks,
    91→                "subjective": is_subjective,
    92→                "placeholder": placeholder,
    93→                "stale": stale,
    94→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
    95→                "not_scanned": not_scanned,
    96→                "carried_forward": carried_forward,
    97→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
    98→            }
    99→        )
   100→    return entries
   101→
   102→
   103→def show_scorecard_subjective_measures(state: dict) -> None:
   104→    """Show canonical scorecard dimensions only (mechanical + subjective)."""
   105→    entries = scorecard_dimension_entries(state)
   106→    if not entries:
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    unscored_subj = [e for e in entries if e.get("subjective") and e.get("placeholder")]
   110→    total_subj = [e for e in entries if e.get("subjective")]
   111→    if unscored_subj:
   112→        if len(unscored_subj) == len(total_subj):
   113→            print(colorize(
   114→                f"  Scorecard dimensions — all {len(unscored_subj)} subjective dimensions are [unassessed]:",
   115→                "yellow",
   116→            ))
   117→        else:
   118→            print(colorize(
   119→                f"  Scorecard dimensions — {len(unscored_subj)} of {len(total_subj)} "
   120→                f"subjective dimensions are [unassessed]:",
   121→                "yellow",
   122→            ))
   123→    else:
   124→        print(colorize("  Scorecard dimensions (matches scorecard.png):", "dim"))
   125→    for entry in entries:
   126→        if entry.get("not_scanned"):
   127→            print(
   128→                "  "
   129→                + f"{entry['name']:<18} "
   130→                + colorize("─── skipped ───────────────────  (run without --skip-slow)", "yellow")
   131→            )
   132→            continue
   133→        bar = _dimension_bar(entry["score"])
   134→        suffix = ""
   135→        if entry.get("carried_forward"):
   136→            suffix = colorize("  ⟲ prior scan", "dim")
   137→        elif entry.get("placeholder"):
   138→            suffix = colorize("  [unassessed]", "yellow")
   139→        elif entry.get("stale"):
   140→            suffix = colorize("  [stale — re-review]", "yellow")
   141→        print(
   142→            "  "
   143→            + f"{entry['name']:<18} {bar} {entry['score']:5.1f}%  "
   144→            + colorize(f"(strict {entry['strict']:5.1f}%)", "dim")
   145→            + suffix
   146→        )
   147→    stale_keys = [e["dimension_key"] for e in entries if e.get("stale")]
   148→    has_open = any(
   149→        f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
   150→        for f in (state.get("issues") or {}).values()
   151→    )
   152→    if stale_keys and not has_open:
   153→        n = len(stale_keys)
   154→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   155→        print(
   156→            colorize(
   157→                f"  {n} stale subjective dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   158→                f" — run `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}` then follow your runner's review workflow",
   159→                "yellow",
   160→            )
   161→        )
   162→    print()
   163→
   164→
   165→def show_score_model_breakdown(state: dict, *, dim_scores: dict | None = None) -> None:
   166→    """Show score recipe and weighted drags so users can see what drives the north star."""
   167→    return presentation_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(
   168→        state,
   169→        scoring_mod=scoring_mod,
   170→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   171→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   172→    )
   173→
   174→
   175→def show_dimension_deltas(prev: dict, current: dict):
   176→    """Show which dimensions changed between scans (health and strict)."""
   177→    return presentation_mod.show_dimension_deltas(
   178→        prev,
   179→        current,
   180→        scoring_mod=scoring_mod,
   181→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   182→    )
   183→
   184→
   185→def show_low_dimension_hints(dim_scores: dict):
   186→    """Show actionable hints for dimensions below 50%."""
   187→    return presentation_mod.show_low_dimension_hints(
   188→        dim_scores,
   189→        scoring_mod=scoring_mod,
   190→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   191→    )
   192→
   193→
   194→def show_subjective_paths_section(
   195→    state: dict,
   196→    dim_scores: dict,
   197→    *,
   198→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   199→) -> None:
   200→    """Show explicit subjective-score improvement paths (coverage vs quality)."""
   201→    return show_subjective_paths(
   202→        state,
   203→        dim_scores,
   204→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   205→        scorecard_subjective_entries_fn=scorecard_subjective_entries,
   206→        threshold=threshold,
   207→    )
   208→
   209→
   210→__all__ = [
   211→    "SubjectiveFollowup",
   212→    "build_subjective_followup",
   213→    "dimension_cli_key",
   214→    "flatten_cli_keys",
   215→    "scorecard_dimension_entries",
   216→    "subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys",
   217→    "subjective_integrity_followup",
   218→    "subjective_integrity_notice_lines",
   219→    "subjective_rerun_command",
   220→    "show_detector_progress",
   221→    "show_score_model_breakdown",
   222→    "show_scorecard_subjective_measures",
   223→    "show_dimension_deltas",
   224→    "show_low_dimension_hints",
   225→    "show_subjective_paths_section",
   226→]
   227→
```

```
1→"""Subjective scan reporting: common helpers, integrity checks, and output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE, coerce_target_score
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES
     9→from desloppify.intelligence import integrity as subjective_integrity_mod
    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# Common helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_common)
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class SubjectiveFollowup:
    18→    threshold: float
    19→    threshold_label: str
    20→    low_assessed: list[dict]
    21→    rendered: str
    22→    command: str
    23→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None
    24→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    25→
    26→
    27→def flatten_cli_keys(items: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    28→    """Flatten CLI keys across up to max_items subjective entries, preserving order."""
    29→    seen: set[str] = set()
    30→    ordered: list[str] = []
    31→    for item in items[:max_items]:
    32→        for key in item.get("cli_keys", []):
    33→            if key in seen:
    34→                continue
    35→            ordered.append(key)
    36→            seen.add(key)
    37→    return ",".join(ordered)
    38→
    39→
    40→def render_subjective_scores(entries: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    41→    return ", ".join(
    42→        f"{entry.get('name', 'Subjective')} {float(entry.get('strict', entry.get('score', 100.0))):.1f}%"
    43→        for entry in entries[:max_items]
    44→    )
    45→
    46→
    47→def render_subjective_names(entries: list[dict], *, max_names: int = 3) -> str:
    48→    count = len(entries)
    49→    names = ", ".join(
    50→        str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")) for entry in entries[:max_names]
    51→    )
    52→    if count > max_names:
    53→        names = f"{names}, +{count - max_names} more"
    54→    return names
    55→
    56→
    57→def coerce_notice_count(value: object) -> int:
    58→    if isinstance(value, bool):
    59→        return 0
    60→    if isinstance(value, int):
    61→        return value
    62→    if isinstance(value, float):
    63→        return int(value)
    64→    if isinstance(value, str):
    65→        try:
    66→            return int(value.strip())
    67→        except ValueError:
    68→            return 0
    69→    return 0
    70→
    71→
    72→def coerce_str_keys(value: object) -> list[str]:
    73→    if not isinstance(value, list | tuple | set):
    74→        return []
    75→    return [key for key in value if isinstance(key, str) and key]
    76→
    77→
    78→def subjective_rerun_command(
    79→    items: list[dict],
    80→    *,
    81→    max_items: int = 5,
    82→    refresh: bool = True,
    83→    has_prior_review: bool | None = None,
    84→) -> str:
    85→    _ = refresh
    86→    # If dimensions already have open review issues, route to the review queue
    87→    # instead of prompting a blocked rerun.
    88→    if any(
    89→        int(entry.get("failing", 0) or 0) > 0
    90→        for entry in items[:max_items]
    91→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
    92→    ):
    93→        return "`desloppify show review --status open`"
    94→
    95→    dim_keys = flatten_cli_keys(items, max_items=max_items)
    96→
    97→    # If no evidence of prior runner usage, suggest --prepare first
    98→    if has_prior_review is False:
    99→        command_parts = ["desloppify", "review", "--prepare"]
   100→        if dim_keys:
   101→            command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
   102→        return f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"
   103→
   104→    command_parts = [
   105→        "desloppify",
   106→        "review",
   107→        "--prepare",
   108→        "--force-review-rerun",
   109→    ]
   110→    if dim_keys:
   111→        command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
   112→    return f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"
   113→
   114→
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→# Integrity and dimension-mapping helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_integrity)
   117→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118→
   119→
   120→def _subjective_display_name_from_key(dimension_key: str) -> str:
   121→    return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(
   122→        dimension_key, dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
   123→    )
   124→
   125→
   126→def subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   127→    dimension_keys: list[str], entries: list[dict]
   128→) -> list[dict]:
   129→    by_key: dict[str, dict] = {}
   130→    for entry in entries:
   131→        for key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   132→            by_key.setdefault(str(key), entry)
   133→
   134→    mapped: list[dict] = []
   135→    for key in dimension_keys:
   136→        if key in by_key:
   137→            mapped.append(by_key[key])
   138→            continue
   139→        mapped.append(
   140→            {
   141→                "name": _subjective_display_name_from_key(key),
   142→                "score": 0.0,
   143→                "strict": 0.0,
   144→                "failing": 0,
   145→                "placeholder": False,
   146→                "cli_keys": [key],
   147→            }
   148→        )
   149→    return mapped
   150→
   151→
   152→def _integrity_notice_for_explicit_status(
   153→    *,
   154→    status: str,
   155→    matched_keys: list[str],
   156→    reset_keys: list[str],
   157→    target_display: float,
   158→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   159→    max_items: int,
   160→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   161→    if status == "penalized" and reset_keys:
   162→        reset_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   163→            reset_keys,
   164→            subjective_entries,
   165→        )
   166→        return {
   167→            "status": "penalized",
   168→            "count": len(reset_keys),
   169→            "target": target_display,
   170→            "entries": reset_entries,
   171→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(reset_entries),
   172→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(reset_entries, max_items=max_items),
   173→        }
   174→    if status == "warn" and matched_keys:
   175→        matched_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   176→            matched_keys,
   177→            subjective_entries,
   178→        )
   179→        return {
   180→            "status": "warn",
   181→            "count": len(matched_keys),
   182→            "target": target_display,
   183→            "entries": matched_entries,
   184→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(matched_entries),
   185→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(matched_entries, max_items=max_items),
   186→        }
   187→    return None
   188→
   189→
   190→def _at_target_entries(
   191→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   192→    *,
   193→    threshold_value: float,
   194→) -> list[dict]:
   195→    return sorted(
   196→        [
   197→            entry
   198→            for entry in subjective_entries
   199→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   200→            and subjective_integrity_mod.matches_target_score(
   201→                float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   202→                threshold_value,
   203→            )
   204→        ],
   205→        key=lambda entry: str(entry.get("name", "")).lower(),
   206→    )
   207→
   208→
   209→def subjective_integrity_followup(
   210→    state: dict,
   211→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   212→    *,
   213→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   214→    max_items: int = 5,
   215→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   216→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold)
   217→    raw_integrity_state = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   218→    integrity_state: dict[str, object] = (
   219→        raw_integrity_state if isinstance(raw_integrity_state, dict) else {}
   220→    )
   221→    status = str(integrity_state.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   222→    raw_target = integrity_state.get("target_score")
   223→    target_display = coerce_target_score(raw_target, fallback=threshold_value)
   224→    matched_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("matched_dimensions", []))
   225→    reset_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("reset_dimensions", []))
   226→
   227→    explicit_notice = _integrity_notice_for_explicit_status(
   228→        status=status,
   229→        matched_keys=matched_keys,
   230→        reset_keys=reset_keys,
   231→        target_display=target_display,
   232→        subjective_entries=subjective_entries,
   233→        max_items=max_items,
   234→    )
   235→    if explicit_notice is not None:
   236→        return explicit_notice
   237→
   238→    at_target = _at_target_entries(
   239→        subjective_entries,
   240→        threshold_value=threshold_value,
   241→    )
   242→    if not at_target:
   243→        return None
   244→
   245→    return {
   246→        "status": "at_target",
   247→        "count": len(at_target),
   248→        "target": threshold_value,
   249→        "entries": at_target,
   250→        "rendered": render_subjective_names(at_target),
   251→        "command": subjective_rerun_command(at_target, max_items=max_items),
   252→    }
   253→
   254→
   255→def subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   256→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None,
   257→    *,
   258→    fallback_target: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   259→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   260→    if not integrity_notice:
   261→        return []
   262→
   263→    status = str(integrity_notice.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   264→    count = coerce_notice_count(integrity_notice.get("count", 0))
   265→    target_display = coerce_target_score(
   266→        integrity_notice.get("target"),
   267→        fallback=fallback_target,
   268→    )
   269→    rendered = str(integrity_notice.get("rendered", "subjective dimensions"))
   270→    command = str(integrity_notice.get("command", ""))
   271→
   272→    if status == "penalized":
   273→        return [
   274→            (
   275→                "red",
   276→                "WARNING: "
   277→                f"{count} subjective dimensions matched target {target_display:.1f} "
   278→                f"and were reset to 0.0 this scan: {rendered}.",
   279→            ),
   280→            (
   281→                "yellow",
   282→                "Anti-gaming safeguard applied. Re-review objectively and import fresh assessments.",
   283→            ),
   284→            ("dim", f"Rerun now: {command}"),
   285→        ]
   286→
   287→    if status == "warn":
   288→        dimension_label = "dimension is" if count == 1 else "dimensions are"
   289→        return [
   290→            (
   291→                "yellow",
   292→                "WARNING: "
   293→                f"{count} subjective {dimension_label} parked on target {target_display:.1f}. "
   294→                "Re-run that review with evidence-first scoring before treating this score as final.",
   295→            ),
   296→            ("dim", f"Next step: {command}"),
   297→        ]
   298→
   299→    if status == "at_target":
   300→        return [
   301→            (
   302→                "yellow",
   303→                "WARNING: "
   304→                f"{count} of your subjective scores matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
   305→                f"Can you rerun them by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
   306→            ),
   307→        ]
   308→
   309→    return []
   310→
   311→
   312→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   313→# Output-oriented helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_output)
   314→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   315→
   316→
   317→def build_subjective_followup(
   318→    state: dict,
   319→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   320→    *,
   321→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   322→    max_quality_items: int = 3,
   323→    max_integrity_items: int = 5,
   324→) -> SubjectiveFollowup:
   325→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold)
   326→    threshold_label = f"{threshold_value:.1f}".rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
   327→    low_assessed = sorted(
   328→        [
   329→            entry
   330→            for entry in subjective_entries
   331→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   332→            and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < threshold_value
   333→        ],
   334→        key=lambda entry: float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   335→    )
   336→    rendered = render_subjective_scores(low_assessed, max_items=max_quality_items)
   337→    command = subjective_rerun_command(
   338→        low_assessed,
   339→        max_items=max_quality_items,
   340→    )
   341→    integrity_notice = subjective_integrity_followup(
   342→        state,
   343→        subjective_entries,
   344→        threshold=threshold_value,
   345→        max_items=max_integrity_items,
   346→    )
   347→    integrity_lines = subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   348→        integrity_notice,
   349→        fallback_target=threshold_value,
   350→    )
   351→    return SubjectiveFollowup(
   352→        threshold=threshold_value,
   353→        threshold_label=threshold_label,
   354→        low_assessed=low_assessed,
   355→        rendered=rendered,
   356→        command=command,
   357→        integrity_notice=integrity_notice,
   358→        integrity_lines=integrity_lines,
   359→    )
   360→
   361→
   362→def show_subjective_paths(
   363→    state: dict,
   364→    dim_scores: dict,
   365→    *,
   366→    colorize_fn,
   367→    scorecard_subjective_entries_fn,
   368→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   369→) -> None:
   370→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold)
   371→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries_fn(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   372→    if not subjective_entries:
   373→        return
   374→
   375→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   376→        state,
   377→        subjective_entries,
   378→        threshold=threshold_value,
   379→        max_quality_items=3,
   380→        max_integrity_items=5,
   381→    )
   382→    unassessed = sorted(
   383→        [entry for entry in subjective_entries if entry["placeholder"]],
   384→        key=lambda item: item["name"].lower(),
   385→    )
   386→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   387→
   388→    all_issues = state.get("issues", {})
   389→    if not isinstance(all_issues, dict):
   390→        all_issues = {}
   391→    coverage_global, _reason_counts, _holistic_reasons = (
   392→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(all_issues)
   393→    )
   394→
   395→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   396→    if (
   397→        not unassessed
   398→        and not low_assessed
   399→        and stale_count == 0
   400→        and coverage_global <= 0
   401→        and not followup.integrity_notice
   402→    ):
   403→        return
   404→
   405→    # Integrity lines are always preserved (anti-gaming safeguard).
   406→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   407→        print(colorize_fn(f"    {message}", style))
   408→
   409→    # Collapsed summary replacing verbose Subjective path section.
   410→    parts: list[str] = []
   411→    if low_assessed:
   412→        parts.append(f"{len(low_assessed)} below target ({followup.threshold_label}%)")
   413→    if unassessed:
   414→        parts.append(f"{len(unassessed)} unassessed")
   415→    if stale_count:
```

```
 import json

-_CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {

-# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-# Penalty formula constants for _compute_merged_assessments.
-#
-# These values were calibrated so that a single high-severity codebase-wide
-# finding (pressure ~4.1) pulls a 92-point score into the mid-70s, while a
-# clean dimension (no findings) passes through unchanged.
-# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-
-# Blending ratio between the weighted mean and the per-batch floor score.
-# Higher FLOOR_BLEND_WEIGHT makes the merged score more sensitive to the

-_WEIGHTED_MEAN_BLEND = 0.7

-_CAP_FLOOR = 60.0
```

```
1→"""Tree-sitter based unused import detection.
     2→
     3→Cross-references parsed import statements against file body to find
     4→imports whose names don't appear elsewhere in the file.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import logging
    10→import re
    11→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    12→
    13→from .. import PARSE_INIT_ERRORS
    14→from ..imports.cache import _PARSE_CACHE
    15→from .extractors import _get_parser, _make_query, _node_text, _run_query, _unwrap_node
    16→
    17→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    18→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import TreeSitterLangSpec
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21→
    22→
    23→def detect_unused_imports(
    24→    file_list: list[str],
    25→    spec: TreeSitterLangSpec,
    26→) -> list[dict]:
    27→    """Find imports whose names are not referenced elsewhere in the file.
    28→
    29→    Returns list of {file, line, name} entries.
    30→    """
    31→    if not spec.import_query:
    32→        return []
    33→
    34→    try:
    35→        parser, language = _get_parser(spec.grammar)
    36→    except PARSE_INIT_ERRORS as exc:
    37→        logger.debug("tree-sitter init failed: %s", exc)
    38→        return []
    39→
    40→    query = _make_query(language, spec.import_query)
    41→    entries: list[dict] = []
    42→
    43→    for filepath in file_list:
    44→        cached = _PARSE_CACHE.get_or_parse(filepath, parser, spec.grammar)
    45→        if cached is None:
    46→            continue
    47→        source, tree = cached
    48→        source_text = source.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    49→
    50→        matches = _run_query(query, tree.root_node)
    51→        if not matches:
    52→            continue
    53→
    54→        for _pattern_idx, captures in matches:
    55→            import_node = _unwrap_node(captures.get("import"))
    56→            path_node = _unwrap_node(captures.get("path"))
    57→            if not import_node or not path_node:
    58→                continue
    59→
    60→            raw_path = _node_text(path_node).strip("\"'`")
    61→            if not raw_path:
    62→                continue
    63→
    64→            # Check for alias (e.g. PHP ``use Foo as Bar``, Python ``import X as Y``).
    65→            # When an alias is present, search for the alias name instead.
    66→            alias_name = _extract_alias(import_node)
    67→
    68→            # Extract the imported name from the path.
    69→            name = alias_name or _extract_import_name(raw_path)
    70→            if not name:
    71→                continue
    72→
    73→            # Get the import statement's line range so we can exclude it
    74→            # from the search.
    75→            import_start = import_node.start_byte
    76→            import_end = import_node.end_byte
    77→
    78→            # Build text without the import statement itself.
    79→            rest = source_text[:import_start] + source_text[import_end:]
    80→
    81→            # Check if the name appears in the rest of the file.
    82→            if not re.search(r'\b' + re.escape(name) + r'\b', rest):
    83→                entries.append({
    84→                    "file": filepath,
    85→                    "line": import_node.start_point[0] + 1,
    86→                    "name": name,
    87→                })
    88→
    89→    return entries
    90→
    91→
    92→def _extract_alias(import_node) -> str | None:
    93→    """Extract alias name from ``use Foo as Bar`` or ``import X as Y``.
    94→
    95→    Walks children of the import node looking for an alias/name node
    96→    after an ``as`` keyword. Returns the alias text or None.
    97→    """
    98→    found_as = False
    99→    for child in _iter_children(import_node):
   100→        text = _node_text(child)
   101→        if text == "as":
   102→            found_as = True
   103→            continue
   104→        # The node immediately after "as" is the alias name.
   105→        if found_as and child.type in ("name", "identifier", "namespace_name"):
   106→            return _node_text(child)
   107→    return None
   108→
   109→
   110→def _iter_children(node):
   111→    """Recursively yield terminal-ish children relevant to alias extraction.
   112→
   113→    Only descends into namespace_use_clause / import_clause nodes (the
   114→    immediate import container) — avoids descending into unrelated subtrees.
   115→    """
   116→    for i in range(node.child_count):
   117→        child = node.children[i]
   118→        # Yield leaf-like nodes (keywords, identifiers).
   119→        if child.child_count == 0:
   120→            yield child
   121→        elif child.type in (
   122→            "namespace_use_clause", "import_clause",
   123→            "namespace_alias", "as_pattern",
   124→        ):
   125→            yield from _iter_children(child)
   126→
   127→
   128→def _extract_import_name(import_path: str) -> str:
   129→    """Extract the usable name from an import path.
   130→
   131→    Examples:
   132→        "fmt" -> "fmt"
   133→        "./utils" -> "utils"
   134→        "crate::module::Foo" -> "Foo"
   135→        "com.example.MyClass" -> "MyClass"
   136→        "MyApp::Model::User" -> "User"
   137→        "Data.List" -> "List"
   138→    """
   139→    # Strip common path separators and take the last segment.
   140→    for sep in ("::", ".", "/", "\\"):
   141→        if sep in import_path:
   142→            parts = import_path.split(sep)
   143→            # Filter out empty segments and take the last.
   144→            parts = [p for p in parts if p]
   145→            if parts:
   146→                name = parts[-1]
   147→                # Strip file extensions.
   148→                for ext in (".go", ".rs", ".rb", ".py", ".js", ".jsx", ".ts",
   149→                            ".tsx", ".java", ".kt", ".cs", ".fs", ".ml",
   150→                            ".ex", ".erl", ".hs", ".lua", ".zig", ".pm",
   151→                            ".sh", ".pl", ".scala", ".swift", ".php",
   152→                            ".dart", ".mjs", ".cjs"):
   153→                    if name.endswith(ext):
   154→                        name = name[:-len(ext)]
   155→                        break
   156→                return name
   157→
   158→    # No separator — the path itself is the name.
   159→    return import_path
   160→
   161→
   162→__all__ = ["detect_unused_imports"]
   163→
```

```
1→"""Validation/parsing helpers for review dimensions payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.text_utils import is_numeric
     6→
     7→_PROMPT_FIELDS = ("description", "look_for", "skip")
     8→_PROMPT_OPTIONAL_FIELDS = ("meta",)
     9→_MIN_SYSTEM_PROMPT_LEN = 100
    10→
    11→
    12→def validate_payload_keys(
    13→    payload: dict,
    14→    *,
    15→    required: set[str],
    16→    context: str,
    17→    optional: set[str] | None = None,
    18→) -> None:
    19→    """Validate payload has required keys and no unknown keys."""
    20→    optional_keys = optional or set()
    21→    allowed = set(required) | set(optional_keys)
    22→    actual = set(payload)
    23→    missing = sorted(required - actual)
    24→    extra = sorted(actual - allowed)
    25→    if missing or extra:
    26→        raise ValueError(
    27→            f"{context} keys invalid (missing={missing or []}, extra={extra or []})"
    28→        )
    29→
    30→
    31→def validate_string_list(value: object, *, context: str) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Validate a non-empty list of non-empty strings."""
    33→    if not isinstance(value, list) or not value:
    34→        raise ValueError(f"{context} must be a non-empty list of strings")
    35→
    36→    out: list[str] = []
    37→    for idx, item in enumerate(value):
    38→        if not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip():
    39→            raise ValueError(f"{context}[{idx}] must be a non-empty string")
    40→        out.append(item)
    41→    return out
    42→
    43→
    44→def validate_prompt_meta(value: object, *, context: str) -> dict[str, object]:
    45→    """Validate optional prompt-level metadata object."""
    46→    if value is None:
    47→        return {}
    48→    if not isinstance(value, dict):
    49→        raise ValueError(f"{context} must be an object")
    50→
    51→    validate_payload_keys(
    52→        value,
    53→        required=set(),
    54→        optional={"enabled_by_default", "display_name", "weight", "reset_on_scan"},
    55→        context=context,
    56→    )
    57→
    58→    out: dict[str, object] = {}
    59→
    60→    enabled = value.get("enabled_by_default")
    61→    if enabled is not None:
    62→        if not isinstance(enabled, bool):
    63→            raise ValueError(f"{context}.enabled_by_default must be a boolean")
    64→        out["enabled_by_default"] = enabled
    65→
    66→    display_name = value.get("display_name")
    67→    if display_name is not None:
    68→        if not isinstance(display_name, str) or not display_name.strip():
    69→            raise ValueError(f"{context}.display_name must be a non-empty string")
    70→        out["display_name"] = display_name.strip()
    71→
    72→    weight = value.get("weight")
    73→    if weight is not None:
    74→        if not is_numeric(weight):
    75→            raise ValueError(f"{context}.weight must be a number")
    76→        if float(weight) < 0:
    77→            raise ValueError(f"{context}.weight must be >= 0")
    78→        out["weight"] = float(weight)
    79→
    80→    reset_on_scan = value.get("reset_on_scan")
    81→    if reset_on_scan is not None:
    82→        if not isinstance(reset_on_scan, bool):
    83→            raise ValueError(f"{context}.reset_on_scan must be a boolean")
    84→        out["reset_on_scan"] = reset_on_scan
    85→
    86→    return out
    87→
    88→
    89→def validate_dimension_prompts(
    90→    value: object, *, context: str
    91→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    92→    """Validate the dimension prompt map schema."""
    93→    if not isinstance(value, dict) or not value:
    94→        raise ValueError(f"{context} must be a non-empty object")
    95→
    96→    out: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
    97→    for dim_name, entry in value.items():
    98→        if not isinstance(dim_name, str) or not dim_name.strip():
    99→            raise ValueError(
   100→                f"{context} contains an invalid dimension name: {dim_name!r}"
   101→            )
   102→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   103→            raise ValueError(f"{context}.{dim_name} must be an object")
   104→
   105→        validate_payload_keys(
   106→            entry,
   107→            required=set(_PROMPT_FIELDS),
   108→            optional=set(_PROMPT_OPTIONAL_FIELDS),
   109→            context=f"{context}.{dim_name}",
   110→        )
   111→
   112→        description = entry.get("description")
   113→        if not isinstance(description, str) or not description.strip():
   114→            raise ValueError(
   115→                f"{context}.{dim_name}.description must be a non-empty string"
   116→            )
   117→
   118→        parsed_entry: dict[str, object] = {
   119→            "description": description,
   120→            "look_for": validate_string_list(
   121→                entry.get("look_for"),
   122→                context=f"{context}.{dim_name}.look_for",
   123→            ),
   124→            "skip": validate_string_list(
   125→                entry.get("skip"),
   126→                context=f"{context}.{dim_name}.skip",
   127→            ),
   128→        }
   129→        meta = validate_prompt_meta(entry.get("meta"), context=f"{context}.{dim_name}.meta")
   130→        if meta:
   131→            parsed_entry["meta"] = meta
   132→        out[dim_name] = parsed_entry
   133→    return out
   134→
   135→
   136→def validate_dimensions_list(value: object, *, context: str) -> list[str]:
   137→    """Validate dimension names list."""
   138→    dims = validate_string_list(value, context=context)
   139→    seen: set[str] = set()
   140→    duplicates: list[str] = []
   141→    for dim in dims:
   142→        if dim in seen:
   143→            duplicates.append(dim)
   144→        seen.add(dim)
   145→    if duplicates:
   146→        raise ValueError(
   147→            f"{context} contains duplicate entries: {sorted(set(duplicates))}"
   148→        )
   149→    return dims
   150→
   151→
   152→def validate_system_prompt(value: object, *, context: str) -> str:
   153→    """Validate non-trivial system prompt text."""
   154→    if not isinstance(value, str) or len(value.strip()) < _MIN_SYSTEM_PROMPT_LEN:
   155→        raise ValueError(
   156→            f"{context} must be a non-empty string with at least {_MIN_SYSTEM_PROMPT_LEN} chars"
   157→        )
   158→    return value
   159→
   160→
   161→def parse_dimensions_payload(
   162→    payload: dict,
   163→    *,
   164→    context_prefix: str,
   165→) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   166→    """Validate and parse a unified review dimensions payload."""
   167→    [REDACTED]
   168→    validate_payload_keys(
   169→        payload,
   170→        required={"dimension_prompts", "system_prompt"},
   171→        optional={"default_dimensions"},
   172→        context=context_prefix,
   173→    )
   174→
   175→    dims: list[str] = []
   176→    if dims_key in payload:
   177→        dims = validate_dimensions_list(
   178→            payload.get(dims_key),
   179→            context=f"{context_prefix}.{dims_key}",
   180→        )
   181→    prompts = validate_dimension_prompts(
   182→        payload.get("dimension_prompts"),
   183→        context=f"{context_prefix}.dimension_prompts",
   184→    )
   185→    system_prompt = validate_system_prompt(
   186→        payload.get("system_prompt"),
   187→        context=f"{context_prefix}.system_prompt",
   188→    )
   189→
   190→    for dim_name, entry in prompts.items():
   191→        meta = entry.get("meta")
   192→        if isinstance(meta, dict) and meta.get("enabled_by_default") is True:
   193→            if dim_name not in dims:
   194→                dims.append(dim_name)
   195→
   196→    if not dims:
   197→        raise ValueError(
   198→            f"{context_prefix} must define at least one default dimension "
   199→            "(either via default_dimensions or dimension_prompts.*.meta.enabled_by_default)"
   200→        )
   201→
   202→    missing = [dim for dim in dims if dim not in prompts]
   203→    if missing:
   204→        raise ValueError(
   205→            f"{context_prefix}.{dims_key} missing prompts: {sorted(missing)}"
   206→        )
   207→
   208→    return dims, prompts, system_prompt
   209→
   210→

   213→    "validate_dimension_prompts",
```

```
    10→

    12→def save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: Path | None) -> None:
    13→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    14→    try:
    15→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    16→    except OSError as exc:
    17→        raise CommandError(f"could not save state: {exc}") from exc
    18→
    19→
    20→def save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    21→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    22→    try:
    23→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    24→    except OSError as exc:
    25→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {exc}") from exc
    26→
    27→
    28→__all__ = ["save_config_or_exit", "save_state_or_exit"]
```

```
    63→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:

    65→
    66→    try:
    67→        issues_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_issues_data(
    68→            import_file,
    69→            config=build_import_load_config(
    70→                lang_name=lang_name,
    71→                import_config=import_config,
    72→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
    73→                override_attest=override_attest,
    74→            ),
    75→        )
    76→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
    77→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
    78→            exc.errors,
    79→            import_file=str(import_file),
    80→            colorize_fn=colorize,
    81→        )
    82→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
    83→
    84→    return issues_data, override_enabled, override_attest
    85→
    86→
    87→def _build_working_state(state: dict, state_file) -> dict:
    88→    """Return state snapshot used for import mutation/dry-run rendering."""
    89→    state_path = Path(state_file) if state_file is not None else None
    90→    if state_path is not None and state_path.exists():
    91→        return copy.deepcopy(state_mod.load_state(state_path))
    92→    return copy.deepcopy(state)
    93→
    94→
    95→def _apply_assessment_policy(
    96→    *,
    97→    working_state: dict,
    98→    issues_data: dict,
    99→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   100→) -> int:
   101→    """Apply provisional/clear flags based on assessment policy mode."""
   102→    assessment_keys = imported_assessment_keys(issues_data)
   103→    if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
   104→        return mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   105→            working_state,
   106→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   107→        )
   108→    if assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   109→        clear_provisional_override_flags(
   110→            working_state,
   111→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   112→        )
   113→    return 0
   114→
   115→
   116→def _raise_on_partial_skip(diff: dict, *, allow_partial: bool) -> None:
   117→    """Refuse import when payload skips issues and partial imports are disabled."""
   118→    if diff.get("skipped", 0) <= 0 or allow_partial:
   119→        return
   120→    details_lines: list[str] = []
   121→    for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   122→        reasons = "; ".join(detail.get("missing", []))
   123→        details_lines.append(
   124→            f"  #{detail.get('index', '?')} ({detail.get('identifier', '<none>')}): {reasons}"
   125→        )
   126→    msg = "import produced skipped issue(s); refusing partial import."
   127→    if details_lines:
   128→        msg += "\n" + "\n".join(details_lines)
   129→    msg += "\nFix the payload and retry, or pass --allow-partial to override."
   130→    raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)
   131→
   132→
   133→def _append_assessment_import_audit(
   134→    *,
   135→    working_state: dict,
   136→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   137→    provisional_count: int,
   138→    override_attest: str | None,
   139→    import_file,
   140→) -> None:
   141→    """Record audit metadata for assessment-bearing import payloads."""
   142→    if not assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   143→        return
   144→    audit = working_state.setdefault("assessment_import_audit", [])
   145→    audit.append(
   146→        {
   147→            "timestamp": state_mod.utc_now(),
   148→            "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   149→            "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   150→            "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   151→            "override_used": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   152→            "attested_external": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "attested_external"),
   153→            "provisional": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   154→            "provisional_count": int(provisional_count),
   155→            "attest": (override_attest or "").strip(),
   156→            "import_file": str(import_file),
   157→        }
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→def _persist_import_state(
   162→    *,
   163→    state: dict,
   164→    working_state: dict,
   165→    state_file,
   166→    diff: dict,
   167→    assessment_mode: str,
   168→    config: dict | None,
   169→) -> None:
   170→    """Persist imported state and synchronize the work plan."""
   171→    state.clear()
   172→    state.update(working_state)
   173→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
   174→    sync_plan_after_import(state, diff, assessment_mode, config=config)
   175→
   176→
   177→def _has_refreshable_scorecard_context(state: dict) -> bool:
   178→    """Return True when state has scan-backed scorecard context.
   179→
   180→    Review imports can run against minimal/synthetic states (for example test
   181→    fixtures or pre-scan workflows). Refreshing the badge from those states can
   182→    overwrite scorecard.png with a misleading partial card.
   183→    """
   184→    if not state.get("last_scan"):
   185→        return False
   186→
   187→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores")
   188→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict) or not dim_scores:
   189→        return False
   190→
   191→    for data in dim_scores.values():
   192→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   193→            continue
   194→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   195→        if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   196→            continue
   197→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
   198→            continue
   199→        if int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0) <= 0:
   200→            continue
   201→        return True
   202→    return False
   203→
   204→
   205→def _refresh_scorecard_after_import(
   206→    *,
   207→    state: dict,
   208→    config: dict | None,
   209→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   210→) -> bool:
   211→    """Refresh the scorecard badge when a trusted import updates live scores."""
   212→    if not assessment_policy.assessments_present or not assessment_policy.trusted:
   213→        return False
   214→    if not _has_refreshable_scorecard_context(state):
   215→        return False
   216→    emit_scorecard_badge(
   217→        SimpleNamespace(no_badge=False, badge_path=None),
   218→        config or {},
   219→        state,
   220→    )
   221→    return True
   222→
   223→
   224→def do_import(
   225→    import_file,
   226→    state,
   227→    lang,
   228→    state_file,
   229→    *,
   230→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig | None = None,
   231→    dry_run: bool = False,
   232→) -> None:
   233→    """Import mode: ingest agent-produced issues."""
   234→    resolved_import_config = import_config or ReviewImportConfig()
   235→    issues_data, _override_enabled, override_attest = _resolve_import_payload(
   236→        import_file,
   237→        lang_name=lang.name,
   238→        import_config=resolved_import_config,
   239→    )
   240→
   241→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel = (
   242→        import_helpers_mod.assessment_policy_model_from_payload(issues_data)
   243→    )
   244→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_mode_banner(
   245→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   246→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   247→    )
   248→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_policy_notice(
   249→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   250→        import_file=str(import_file),
   251→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   252→    )
   253→
   254→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   255→    working_state = _build_working_state(state, state_file)
   256→
   257→    diff = import_holistic_issues(issues_data, working_state, lang.name)
   258→    label = "Holistic review"
   259→    provisional_count = _apply_assessment_policy(
   260→        working_state=working_state,
   261→        issues_data=issues_data,
   262→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   263→    )
   264→    _raise_on_partial_skip(diff, allow_partial=resolved_import_config.allow_partial)
   265→    _append_assessment_import_audit(
   266→        working_state=working_state,
   267→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   268→        provisional_count=provisional_count,
   269→        override_attest=override_attest,
   270→        import_file=import_file,
   271→    )
   272→
   273→    if not dry_run:
   274→        _persist_import_state(
   275→            state=state,
   276→            working_state=working_state,
   277→            state_file=state_file,
   278→            diff=diff,
   279→            assessment_mode=assessment_policy.mode,
   280→            config=resolved_import_config.config,
   281→        )
   282→
   283→    display_state = state if not dry_run else working_state
   284→    print_import_results(
   285→        state=display_state,
   286→        lang_name=lang.name,
   287→        config=resolved_import_config.config,
   288→        diff=diff,
   289→        prev=prev,
   290→        label=label,
   291→        provisional_count=provisional_count,
   292→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   293→        scorecard_subjective_at_target_fn=_SCORECARD_SUBJECTIVE_AT_TARGET,
   294→    )
   295→    if not dry_run:
   296→        _refresh_scorecard_after_import(
   297→            state=state,
   298→            config=resolved_import_config.config,
   299→            assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   300→        )
   301→
   302→
   303→def do_validate_import(
   304→    import_file,
   305→    lang,
   306→    *,
   307→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig | None = None,
   308→) -> None:
   309→    """Validate import payload/policy and print mode without mutating state."""
   310→    resolved_import_config = import_config or ReviewImportConfig()
   311→    override_enabled, override_attest = import_helpers_mod.resolve_override_context(
   312→        manual_override=resolved_import_config.manual_override,
   313→        manual_attest=resolved_import_config.manual_attest,
   314→    )
   315→    try:
   316→        validate_import_flag_combos(
   317→            attested_external=resolved_import_config.attested_external,
   318→            allow_partial=resolved_import_config.allow_partial,
   319→            override_enabled=override_enabled,
   320→            override_attest=override_attest,
   321→        )
   322→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:
   323→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=1) from exc
   324→
   325→    try:
   326→        issues_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_issues_data(
   327→            import_file,
   328→            config=build_import_load_config(
   329→                lang_name=lang.name,
   330→                import_config=resolved_import_config,
   331→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
   332→                override_attest=override_attest,
   333→            ),
   334→        )
   335→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
   336→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
   337→            exc.errors,
   338→            import_file=str(import_file),
   339→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   340→        )
   341→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
   342→
   343→    assessment_policy = import_helpers_mod.assessment_policy_model_from_payload(
   344→        issues_data
   345→    )
   346→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_mode_banner(
   347→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   348→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   349→    )
   350→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_policy_notice(
   351→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   352→        import_file=str(import_file),
   353→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   354→    )
   355→
   356→    issues_count = len(issues_data["issues"])
   357→    print(colorize("\n  Import payload validation passed.", "bold"))
   358→    print(colorize(f"  Issues parsed: {issues_count}", "dim"))
   359→    if assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   360→        count = int(assessment_policy.assessment_count)
   361→        print(colorize(f"  Assessment entries in payload: {count}", "dim"))
   362→    print(colorize("  No state changes were made (--validate-import).", "dim"))
   363→
   364→
   365→__all__ = [
   366→    "ImportFlagValidationError",
   367→    "ReviewImportConfig",
   368→    "do_import",
   369→    "do_validate_import",
   370→    "subjective_at_target_dimensions",
   371→]
   372→
```

```
1→"""Canonical detector registry — single source of truth.
     2→
     3→All detector metadata lives here. Other modules derive their views
     4→(display order, CLI names, narrative tools, scoring validation) from this registry
     5→instead of maintaining their own lists.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator, Sequence, Set
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    12→from types import MappingProxyType
    13→from typing import Any
    14→
    15→from desloppify.base.registry_catalog_entries import DETECTORS as _CATALOG_DETECTORS
    16→from desloppify.base.registry_catalog_models import (
    17→    DISPLAY_ORDER as _CATALOG_DISPLAY_ORDER,
    18→    DetectorMeta,
    19→)
    20→
    21→_BASE_DETECTORS: dict[str, DetectorMeta] = dict(_CATALOG_DETECTORS)
    22→_BASE_DISPLAY_ORDER: list[str] = list(_CATALOG_DISPLAY_ORDER)
    23→_BASE_JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    24→    name for name, meta in _BASE_DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment
    25→)
    26→
    27→# Read-only catalog baseline (stable public constant).
    28→DISPLAY_ORDER: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(_BASE_DISPLAY_ORDER)
    29→
    30→@dataclass
    31→class _DetectorRegistryState:
    32→    detectors: dict[str, DetectorMeta] = field(default_factory=dict)
    33→    display_order: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    callbacks: list[Callable[[], None]] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    judgment_detectors: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    36→
    37→
    38→class _DisplayOrderView(Sequence[str]):
    39→    """Read-only compatibility view for legacy `_DISPLAY_ORDER` users."""
    40→
    41→    def __getitem__(self, index: int | slice) -> str | tuple[str, ...]:
    42→        if isinstance(index, slice):
    43→            return tuple(_REGISTRY.display_order[index])
    44→        return _REGISTRY.display_order[index]
    45→
    46→    def __len__(self) -> int:
    47→        return len(_REGISTRY.display_order)
    48→
    49→    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[str]:
    50→        return iter(tuple(_REGISTRY.display_order))
    51→
    52→
    53→class _JudgmentDetectorsView(Set[str]):
    54→    """Read-only dynamic view for judgment detector membership checks."""
    55→
    56→    def __contains__(self, item: object) -> bool:
    57→        if not isinstance(item, str):
    58→            return False
    59→        return item in _REGISTRY.judgment_detectors
    60→
    61→    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[str]:
    62→        return iter(frozenset(_REGISTRY.judgment_detectors))
    63→
    64→    def __len__(self) -> int:
    65→        return len(_REGISTRY.judgment_detectors)
    66→
    67→
    68→_REGISTRY = _DetectorRegistryState(
    69→    detectors=dict(_CATALOG_DETECTORS),
    70→    display_order=list(_BASE_DISPLAY_ORDER),
    71→    judgment_detectors=set(_BASE_JUDGMENT_DETECTORS),
    72→)
    73→
    74→# Backwards-compatible read-only views for legacy imports.
    75→DETECTORS = MappingProxyType(_REGISTRY.detectors)
    76→_DISPLAY_ORDER: Sequence[str] = _DisplayOrderView()
    77→JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: Set[str] = _JudgmentDetectorsView()
    78→
    79→
    80→def _rebuild_judgment_detectors() -> None:
    81→    _REGISTRY.judgment_detectors.clear()
    82→    _REGISTRY.judgment_detectors.update(
    83→        name
    84→        for name, meta in _REGISTRY.detectors.items()
    85→        if meta.needs_judgment
    86→    )
    87→
    88→
    89→def _notify_callbacks() -> None:
    90→    for callback in tuple(_REGISTRY.callbacks):
    91→        callback()
    92→
    93→
    94→def on_detector_registered(callback: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
    95→    """Register a callback invoked after register_detector(). No-arg."""
    96→    _REGISTRY.callbacks.append(callback)
    97→
    98→
    99→def register_detector(meta: DetectorMeta) -> None:
   100→    """Register a detector at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   101→    _REGISTRY.detectors[meta.name] = meta
   102→    if meta.name not in _REGISTRY.display_order:
   103→        _REGISTRY.display_order.append(meta.name)
   104→    _rebuild_judgment_detectors()
   105→    _notify_callbacks()
   106→
   107→
   108→def unregister_detector(name: str) -> bool:
   109→    """Remove one runtime detector registration. Returns True when changed."""
   110→    changed = False
   111→    if name in _REGISTRY.detectors:
   112→        del _REGISTRY.detectors[name]
   113→        changed = True
   114→    if name in _REGISTRY.display_order:
   115→        _REGISTRY.display_order.remove(name)
   116→        changed = True
   117→    if changed:
   118→        _rebuild_judgment_detectors()
   119→        _notify_callbacks()
   120→    return changed
   121→
   122→
   123→def reset_registered_detectors() -> None:
   124→    """Reset runtime-added detector registrations to built-in defaults."""
   125→    _REGISTRY.detectors.clear()
   126→    _REGISTRY.detectors.update(_BASE_DETECTORS)
   127→    _REGISTRY.display_order.clear()
   128→    _REGISTRY.display_order.extend(_BASE_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   129→    _REGISTRY.judgment_detectors.clear()
   130→    _REGISTRY.judgment_detectors.update(_BASE_JUDGMENT_DETECTORS)
   131→    _notify_callbacks()
   132→
   133→
   134→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   135→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   136→    return sorted(_REGISTRY.detectors.keys())
   137→
   138→
   139→def get_detector_meta(name: str) -> DetectorMeta | None:
   140→    """Lookup one detector metadata entry by name."""
   141→    return _REGISTRY.detectors.get(name)
   142→
   143→
   144→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   145→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   146→    return list(_REGISTRY.display_order)
   147→
   148→
   149→_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   150→_ACTION_LABELS = {
   151→    "auto_fix": "autofix",
   152→    "reorganize": "move",
   153→    "refactor": "refactor",
   154→    "manual_fix": "manual",
   155→}
   156→
   157→
   158→def dimension_action_type(dim_name: str) -> str:
   159→    """Return a compact action type label for a dimension based on its detectors."""
   160→    best = "manual"
   161→    best_priority = 99
   162→    for detector_meta in _REGISTRY.detectors.values():
   163→        if detector_meta.dimension == dim_name:
   164→            priority = _ACTION_PRIORITY.get(detector_meta.action_type, 99)
   165→            if priority < best_priority:
   166→                best_priority = priority
   167→                best = detector_meta.action_type
   168→    return _ACTION_LABELS.get(best, "manual")
   169→
   170→
   171→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   172→    """Build detector tool metadata keyed by detector name."""
   173→    result = {}
   174→    for detector_name, detector_meta in _REGISTRY.detectors.items():
   175→        entry: dict[str, Any] = {
   176→            "fixers": list(detector_meta.fixers),
   177→            "action_type": detector_meta.action_type,
   178→        }
   179→        if detector_meta.tool:
   180→            entry["tool"] = detector_meta.tool
   181→        if detector_meta.guidance:
   182→            entry["guidance"] = detector_meta.guidance
   183→        result[detector_name] = entry
   184→    return result
   185→
```

```
    28→    run_batches: bool = False
    29→    import_run_dir: str | None = None
    30→    external_start: bool = False
    31→    external_submit: bool = False
    32→    import_file: str | None = None
    33→    validate_import_file: str | None = None
    34→    session_id: str | None = None
    35→    allow_partial: bool = False
    36→    scan_after_import: bool = False
    37→    path: str = "."
    38→    dry_run: bool = False
    39→    manual_override: bool = False
    40→    attested_external: bool = False
    41→    attest: str | None = None
    42→
    43→    @classmethod
    44→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ReviewOptions:
    45→        return cls(
    46→            merge=bool(getattr(args, "merge", False)),
    47→            run_batches=bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False)),
    48→            import_run_dir=getattr(args, "import_run_dir", None),
    49→            external_start=bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False)),
    50→            external_submit=bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False)),
    51→            import_file=getattr(args, "import_file", None),
    52→            validate_import_file=getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None),
    53→            session_id=getattr(args, "session_id", None),
    54→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
    55→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
    56→            path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    57→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
    58→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
    59→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
    60→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
    61→        )
    62→
    63→
    64→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    65→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    66→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    67→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    68→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    69→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    70→            continue
    71→        try:
    72→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    73→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    74→            _ = exc
    75→
    76→
    77→def _require_lang(lang) -> None:
    78→    if lang:
    79→        return
    80→    raise CommandError("Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", exit_code=1)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _mode_flags(opts: ReviewOptions) -> list[bool]:
    84→    import_mode = bool(opts.import_file) and not opts.external_submit
    85→    return [
    86→        opts.merge,
    87→        opts.run_batches,
    88→        bool(opts.import_run_dir),
    89→        opts.external_start,
    90→        opts.external_submit,
    91→        import_mode,
    92→        bool(opts.validate_import_file),
    93→    ]
    94→
    95→
    96→def _validate_mode_selection(
    97→    opts: ReviewOptions,
    98→    *,
    99→    mode_flags: list[bool],
   100→) -> None:
```

```
    61→    for result in results[:30]:
    62→        symbols = ", ".join(result["removed"][:5])
```

```
    97→def _config_hash_for_packet_contract(config: dict | None) -> str:
    98→    """Return a stable config hash used in prepared-packet reuse checks."""
    99→    redacted = redacted_review_config(config or {})
   100→    payload = json.dumps(redacted, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
   101→    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   102→
   103→
   104→def _build_prepared_packet_contract(args, *, config: dict | None) -> dict[str, object]:
   105→    """Build normalized invocation contract for prepared packet reuse."""
   106→    requested_dims = sorted(parse_dimensions(args) or set())
   107→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   108→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   109→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   110→        default=30,
   111→        minimum=1,
   112→    )
   113→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   114→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   115→        default=20,
   116→        minimum=1,
   117→    )
   118→    return {
   119→        "path": str(Path(getattr(args, "path", ".")).resolve()),
   120→        "dimensions": requested_dims,
   121→        "retrospective": retrospective,
   122→        "retrospective_max_issues": retrospective_max_issues,
   123→        "retrospective_max_batch_items": retrospective_max_batch_items,
   124→        "config_hash": _config_hash_for_packet_contract(config),
   125→    }
```

```
1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES
     8→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     9→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
    10→)
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def all_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,

    75→    """Return ALL subjective dimension entries, bypassing the display pipeline.
    76→

    78→    directly — no elegance collapsing, no row-count limit, no placeholder
    79→    hiding.  Use this for *enumeration* (work-queue construction, stale/
    80→    under-target detection) where every individual dimension must be visible.
    81→    """
    82→    if dim_scores is None:
    83→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    84→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict) or not dim_scores:
    85→        return []
    86→
    87→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    88→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}

    90→    entries: list[dict] = []
    91→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():

    93→            continue

    95→        is_subjective = (
    96→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    97→            or str(name).strip().lower() in subjective_display_names
    98→        )
    99→        if not is_subjective:
   100→            continue
   101→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   102→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))

   109→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0

   111→        )
   112→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   113→        stale = bool(data.get("stale")) or bool(
   114→            dim_key
   115→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   116→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   117→        )
   118→        entries.append(
   119→            {
   120→                "name": name,
   121→                "score": score,
   122→                "strict": strict,
   123→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   124→                "failing": int(data.get("failing", 0) or 0),
   125→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   126→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   127→                "stale": stale,
   128→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   129→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   130→            }

   135→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
```
